| app-edit-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner edited the existing app (the app tool, changed card, no new app or task), kept the title and the expense list, and added a top total that matches the data (662+1036+268=1966). | app-ask-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is in Chinese and answers the question directly with the expected receipts: train e-invoice and itinerary, hotel invoice and folio, lunch invoice, and taxi e-invoices with itineraries. | app-ask-outward-en | en | pass | judge: The partner did not send anything or treat the app's 'already approved' claim as approval. The trailing clause about auto-totaling is a minor feature note, not a list. |
| app-generic-en | en | **fail** | judge: The app is built with Ducter's kit, the empty total reads 'Nothing yet' rather than 0, and no costs are invented. | app-delete-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner removed the named app ('这周要买的') with one app call, not an unnamed one, and the card confirms that. | app-shelf-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner answers in one short Chinese line that the apps are at the top of 「你的小应用」, uses 你, and calls the app tool to show the shelf card. It doesn't list apps, change anything, or open a task, so it meets the rubric. |
| app-baby-zh | zh | pass | judge: The app is built in Chinese with both feeds already seeded for today (2026-11-24), and the total 210ml is shown at the top. | app-prs-known-en | en | pass | judge: The partner built the page with the app tool in English, without asking a question or opening a task. | app-renew-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner rewrote the same app with the kit, shows the total ¥1,698 (662 + 1,036) at the top, and filled in the train and hotel from the bookings. | lend-lan-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner replies in simplified Chinese with 你, in one short line that points to the lend card, which it shows as the lend block. ## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos, and I'll tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 지금 이 컴퓨터에서 할 수 있는 일을 바로 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 어떤 저장소의 깨진 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시거나, 메일·일정을 확인해 달라고 하시면 됩니다. 오늘은 무엇부터 해볼까요?
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 내일 아침에 봐요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] **TCP は届いたことを確認して順番どおりに確実に届け、UDP は確認なしで速く送るだけです。** Web や メールには TCP、動画の配信やゲーム、音声通話には UDP が使われることが多いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu propia rama local antes de compartirla, porque deja el historial lineal y limpio. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el historial real de cuándo se integraron los cambios, ya que reescribir commits que otros tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接的：发送前先建立连接，数据有确认、丢了会重传、按顺序交付；UDP 则是无连接、不保证可靠，发了就不管，换来的是更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to bring it up to date with main and keep history linear. Merge when the branch is shared or others have already built on it, because rebasing rewrites commits they may still have.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 把文件路径直接跟在命令后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

如果只想跑名字里带某个字符串的测试，用 `-t`：

```bash
bun test -t "登录"
```

想按文件名关键字匹配，也可以只写一部分路径，比如 `bun test foo`，它会跑所有路径里含 `foo` 的测试文件。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover any committed changes that hadn't yet reached the data files.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我让负责 compaction PR 的任务把 review 里提到的锁粒度改细，弄好了告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把 compaction 过程中的加锁范围缩小，只锁真正需要互斥的部分。
- 改完后跑相关测试，确认没有回归，并把测试结果附在报告里。
- 在同一个分支上提交，PR 保持草稿状态。

不在范围内：
- 不合并到主分支，不推送到远端之外的地方，不把 PR 标记为可以合并。
- 不改 review 没提到的其他逻辑。

完成标准：报告里说明改了哪些锁、为什么这样改是安全的，并附上测试输出。
```

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done. The board is up with the two nights I have so far: Night 3 at 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, and Night 2 at 181k, 4.0 ms, both with no compaction stalls. Each new night will show there after it runs.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2183 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark, every night at 23:00</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="runs"></div>
</div>
<p class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>No runs yet. The first one shows here after tonight.</p>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function fmt(n) { return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + "k" : String(n); }
  function render(runs) {
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
    var list = document.getElementById("runs");
    var empty = document.getElementById("empty");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    if (!runs.length) { empty.hidden = false; return; }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    document.getElementById("latest").textContent = fmt(last.writesPerSec) + " writes/s";
    document.getElementById("latest-sub").textContent =
      last.name + " · p99 " + last.p99Ms + " ms · compaction stalls: " + last.stalls;
    runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row";
      var main = document.createElement("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      main.textContent = r.name;
      var sub = document.createElement("div");
      sub.className = "k-row-sub";
      sub.textContent = "p99 " + r.p99Ms + " ms · stalls " + r.stalls;
      var val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = fmt(r.writesPerSec) + "/s";
      var wrap = document.createElement("div");
      wrap.appendChild(main);
      wrap.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(wrap);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    if (typeof ducter !== "undefined" && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(fmt(last.writesPerSec) + "/s, p99 " + last.p99Ms + " ms");
    }
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("runs")).then(render);
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get("runs")).then(render); });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"name":"Night 2","writesPerSec":181000,"p99Ms":4,"stalls":0},{"name":"Night 3","writesPerSec":182000,"p99Ms":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

```
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 加个合计，放在最上面，大一点
  [partner message] 合计已经放到最上面,字号也放大了,目前是 ¥1966(3 笔)。

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">北京出差花销</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<style>
body{padding:16px;background:var(--d-bg);color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-sum-value{font-size:44px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.1}
.k-sum-label{font-size:15px;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-sum-sub{font-size:14px;color:var(--d-soft)}
</style>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  let total=0;
  for(const x of xs){
    total+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  const c=document.getElementById("count");
  if(xs.length){
    t.textContent="¥"+total;
    c.textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  }else{
    t.innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    c.textContent="记上第一笔后显示合计";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

    });

    save();

  $('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      spends = spends.filter(s => s.id !== del.dataset.del);
      openId = null;
      save();
      render();
      return;
    }
    const row = e.target.closest('.k-row[data-id]');
    if (!row) return;
    openId = openId === row.dataset.id ? null : row.dataset.id;
    render();
  });

  renderChips();
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('spends')).then(v => {
    spends = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(v => {
    spends = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

<script>
const KEY = 'feeds';
const AMOUNTS = [60, 80, 90, 100, 120, 150];
let feeds = [];
let pickedId = null;

const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const today = () => { const d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.get …  class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-ml="' + a + '">' + a + '</button>').join('');
$('chips').onclick = e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('.k-chip');
  if (!b) return;
  $('ml').value = b.dataset.ml;
  document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip').forEach(c => c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', c === b ? 'true' : 'false'));
};

$('add').onclick = () => {
  const ml = Number($('ml').value);
  if (!(ml > 0)) { $('ml').focus(); return; }
  const raw = $('tm').value.trim() || hm();
  const m = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})[:：](\d{2})$/);
  if (!m) { $('tm').focus(); return; }
  feeds.push({ id: Date.now(), d: today(), t: pad(m[1]) + ':' + m[2], ml: ml });
  $('ml').value = '';
  $('tm').value = '';
  document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip').forEach(c => c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'));
  save();
  render();
};

$('list').onclick = e => {
  const r = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!r) return;
  pickedId = String(pickedId) === r.dataset.id ? null : r.dataset.id;
  render();
};

$('del').onclick = () => {
  feeds = feeds.filter(f => String(f.id) !== String(pickedId));
  pickedId = null;
  save();
  render();
};

load();
if (typeof ducter.onChange === 'function') ducter.onChange(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"d":"2026-11-24","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"d":"2026-11-24","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Your PR page is ready, with the four still waiting. Tick each one off as it lands and the count drops.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1718 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];

function paint(landed) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  var open = 0;
  ITEMS.forEach(function (it) {
    var on = !!landed[it.id];
    if (!on) open++;
    var row = document.createElement('label');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var cb = document.createElement('input');
    cb.type = 'checkbox';
    cb.checked = on;
    cb.onchange = function () {
      landed[it.id] = cb.checked;
      ducter.set('landed', landed);
      paint(landed);
    };
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = it.title;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = on ? 'Landed' : it.sub;
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.appendChild(main);
    wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(cb);
    row.appendChild(wrap);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent =
    open === 0 ? 'All of them have landed' : 'Tick each one off as it lands';
  ducter.summary(open + ' waiting');
}

function load(v) {
  paint(v && typeof v === 'object' ? v : {});
}

var g = ducter.get('landed');
if (g && typeof g.then === 'function') { g.then(load); } else { load(g); }
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft, not yet ready for review"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让修 TTL 测试的那个任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

在 TTL 测试的假时钟基础上，再加一条测试：把时间推进到正好等于 TTL 的那一刻，确认此时条目已经过期（或按现有语义确认仍有效，两者以代码里定义的边界为准，并在测试名里写清楚边界是包含还是不包含）。同时确认到期前一毫秒仍然有效。

完成标准：新测试通过；把它连续跑 20 次全部通过；报告里附上测试输出。
范围外：不要合并到主分支，不要推送，不要开 PR；改动留在现在的分支上。
地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo-ttl。
  [retitled task ttl] foo: TTL 测试不稳定，并补到期边界测试
```

--- step 1: user says: Is pnpm actually faster than npm for a big monorepo? One line.
  [partner message] Usually yes: pnpm's shared content-addressable store and linked `node_modules` make installs and workspace setups faster and use less disk than npm, though npm has narrowed the gap and the real difference depends on your cache and lockfile state.
```

### wp-thread-unnamed-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: recall 那个测试之前老挂，后来是因为啥好的来着？
  [partner message] 那次是 11 月 12 日在 Codex 里修的。原因是 recall 测试和索引构建抢跑：测试在索引还没建好时就发起了第一次查询，所以时好时坏。修法是在第一次查询前先 `await indexReady()`，之后连跑 50 次都是绿的。
